## Extraction (extract.md)

First concrete slice of design.md's extractor pipeline: the decoupled `hiker-extract` crate + sidecar write path + the PDF and website-to-markdown extractors + the governed crawl loop. Sources are viewed in the user's OS apps, not rendered in hiker. The byte→text long tail is the source-plugin surface in `## Plugins`.

**Phase 2 (foundation) landed:** the `hiker-extract` leaf crate (`Extractor` trait, ordered `Registry`, `Extracted` contract with `next_urls`, fallback chain, version-aware `CacheKey`, per-source override, capture-spec frontmatter model + `fill_body`), the sidecar write path with provenance stamping, the `[extract]` config section, and the app-side triggers (Make searchable / open-original-external / auto-glob). A trivial passthrough text extractor exercises the path. The real PDF (Phase 3) and web (Phase 4) extractors are still `planned` below.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `extract-preserve-original` | planned | original source byte-for-byte preserved — never modified/moved/deleted; hiker owns only the sidecar; source deletion orphans the sidecar rather than removing it |
| `extract-crate-decoupled` | done | new leaf crate `hiker-extract` (`hiker-extract/src/lib.rs`), added to workspace `members`; `core` does NOT depend on it (verified — `app` wires it, the sidecar `.md` on disk is the seam, ingested by core's existing indexer). Phase 2 added no format/network deps |
| `extract-registry` | done | `hiker-extract/src/lib.rs:Registry` (`Extractor` trait + `Registry::with_builtins`/`route`/`extract`); routes a `Source` (ext/URL) to the first matching extractor; one module + one registration line per type under `builtin/` |
| `extract-contract-next-urls` | done | `hiker-extract/src/contract.rs:Extracted { markdown, frontmatter, archive, next_urls }`; `next_urls` empty = plain one-page extractor, populated = crawl-capable (frontier loop is a later phase) |
| `extract-fallback-chain` | done | `hiker-extract/src/lib.rs:Registry::extract` tries each match in order; `Ok(None)` advances to the next (the passthrough extractor declines non-UTF-8 via `Ok(None)`); `Err` aborts the chain |
| `extract-per-source-override` | done | `hiker-extract/src/lib.rs:Ctx.pinned_extractor` (set from `hiker.extractor` frontmatter via `capture.rs:CaptureSpec.extractor`); `Registry::candidates` restricts to the pinned name, bypassing match order |
| `extract-version-cache-key` | done | `hiker-extract/src/cache.rs:CacheKey { source_hash (blake3), extractor_name, extractor_version }` + `tag()`; bumping `version()` changes the key — mirrors `embedder-version-tag` |
| `extract-trigger-auto-glob` | done | `[extract].auto_globs` matched by `hiker-extract/src/trigger.rs:matches_any_glob`/`glob_matches` (dependency-free gitignore-style matcher); watcher-driven appear/change hook in `app/src/main.rs:maybe_auto_extract`. GAP: an initial bulk startup pass over already-present auto-globbed sources is a follow-up (they re-extract on next change) |
| `extract-trigger-on-demand` | done | filetree "Make searchable" verb (`app/src/files/sidebar.rs:FileVerb::MakeSearchable`) → `app/src/extract.rs:make_searchable` extracts one file + enqueues its sidecar for indexing |
| `extract-trigger-default-ignore` | done | non-md not matched stays ignored (keeps the unsupported marker); "Open original externally" verb (`app/src/files/sidebar.rs:FileVerb::OpenExternal` → `extract.rs:open_external`) opens via the OS handler |
| `capture-spec-note` | partial | data model + parse/serialize landed (`hiker-extract/src/capture.rs:CaptureSpec` — `hiker.kind: capture`, `capture.mode: clip\|crawl\|feed`, source, extractor pin). GAP: the form-over-frontmatter Run UI is a later phase |
| `capture-fill-body-toggle` | done | `hiker-extract/src/capture.rs:CaptureSpec.fill_body` (default `false`); modeled as the body's link-state switch (false = user-owned/untouched; true = linked extracted sidecar overwritten on re-run). File sidecars stamp `fill_body: true` + `link_state: linked` (`sidecar.rs`) |
| `capture-quick-from-url` | partial | core landed: `hiker-extract/src/scrape.rs:scrape` auto-creates a `mode: clip` note with `fill_body: true` (stamped by the sidecar write path) and runs it immediately — the CLI `hiker scrape` path drives it. GAP: the GUI "New from URL" / "Make searchable" affordance that invokes it is deferred |
| `extract-sidecar-write` | done | `hiker-extract/src/sidecar.rs:SidecarWrite` — `write_file_sidecar` (vault-internal non-md → `<full-source-filename>.md` beside it with `hiker:` source/sha256/mtime/type/storage) + `write_url_clip` (URL → visible `mode: clip` note in `[extract].clip_folder`, `<slugified-title>.md` with `-2`/`-3` collision suffix, URL-path-slug fallback). Atomic write; original untouched. Indexer ingests both as ordinary `.md` |
| `extract-sidecar-provenance` | done | `hiker-extract/src/sidecar.rs:stamp_provenance` writes `hiker.author: imported` + a specific `hiker.provenance` label + the `extract_key` cache tag on every written sidecar |
| `extract-sidecar-linked-state` | planned | a `fill_body: true` body is `linked` (RO, re-extraction overwrites); "Unlink from source" → RW, re-extraction stops; re-link re-extracts behind a confirm. Same control as `capture-fill-body-toggle` |
| `extract-sidecar-tree-hidden` | planned | a sidecar *next to a non-md source* (`*.<ext>.md`) is hidden in Files by default + surfaced via "view extracted text"; standalone capture notes (clips, crawl/feed) are ordinary visible notes |
| `extract-open-original-external` | done | `app/src/extract.rs:open_external`/`launch_os_handler` hands the source to the OS default app (xdg-open / `open` / `cmd start`) — distinct from the existing reveal-in-file-manager; wired as the "Open original externally" filetree verb. No in-app web/PDF renderer |
| `extract-pdf-fast-path` | done | `hiker-extract/src/builtin/mod.rs:PdfExtractor` + `builtin/pdf.rs:run` — pure-Rust `pdf-extract` crate (built on `lopdf`; no `pdftotext`/poppler/pdfium/`*-sys`/`cc` — verified via `cargo tree`, dep confined to `hiker-extract`) pulls the text layer → sidecar. Registered ahead of passthrough in `Registry::with_builtins` so it wins the `.pdf` match. Fixture-tested (`tests/fixtures/text.pdf`) |
| `extract-pdf-scanned-detect` | done | `hiker-extract/src/builtin/pdf.rs:looks_scanned` heuristic — empty/garbage fast-path output (scanned/image-only PDF) returns `Ok(None)` so the fallback chain takes over (`MIN_WORD_CHARS=16` floor + `MIN_PRINTABLE_RATIO=0.6` plausible-text-ratio gate). With no fallback the chain ends `Ok(None)`; the caller records the skip via the Phase-2 per-file skipped-state mechanism (`index.md`). Fixture-tested (`tests/fixtures/scanned.pdf`) |
| `extract-pdf-command-escape` | done | `hiker-extract/src/builtin/mod.rs:CommandExtractor` + `builtin/command.rs:run` — format-agnostic per-glob escape hatch; runs a user-configured command (`{input}`/`{output}` placeholders, no shell interpolation) and captures stdout / output file as the body. Empty output declines (`Ok(None)`); non-zero exit / non-UTF-8 is a hard `Err`. App/cli layer builds these from vault `[[extractor.command]]` config and registers them ahead of the built-ins (wiring is app-layer, not yet landed) |
| `extract-pdf-marker-fallback` | planned | deferred — built-in marker/docling fallback for scanned/complex-layout PDFs behind the fast path |
| `extract-image-ocr` | planned | deferred — OCR (tesseract) image extractor; same registry, separate module |
| `extract-audio-transcribe` | planned | deferred — whisper.cpp audio extractor; same registry, separate module |
| `extract-web-static-fetch` | done | website→md via one blocking HTTP GET (`reqwest` `default-features=false` + `rustls-tls`/`blocking`/`gzip`, NO openssl/native-tls — verified via `cargo tree`; only sys-adjacent transitive is `ring`, rustls's crypto backend); no JavaScript, no headless browser. `hiker-extract/src/builtin/web/mod.rs:WebExtractor` + `fetch`/`http_client`; the pure transform is `extract_with_fetcher` (network-free seam the tests drive with local HTML fixtures). All web deps confined to `hiker-extract`; `core` stays network-free |
| `extract-web-readability` | done | `readable-readability` (markup5ever DOM, pure-Rust, no network of its own — chosen over `readability` which drags a default `reqwest`+native-tls) main-content isolation + `htmd` html→markdown → sidecar body. `hiker-extract/src/builtin/web/content.rs:to_article` (title promoted to frontmatter, `content_len` gate) |
| `extract-web-data-blob` | done | parse (never execute) JSON-LD `Article`/`NewsArticle`/`BlogPosting` (incl. `@graph`) / `__NEXT_DATA__` / `window.__NUXT__` blobs via `scraper` + `serde_json` to reach framework-rendered "SPA" sites without their JS. `hiker-extract/src/builtin/web/datablob.rs:probe`; tried ahead of readability in `web/mod.rs:best_body` (min-body-length gate so a stub doesn't beat readability) |
| `extract-web-archive-singlefile` | partial | hand-rolled native-Rust archiver: strips ALL `<script>`, inlines `<link rel=stylesheet>` + `<img>`/`<source>` subresources as base64 `data:` URIs via an injected fetcher (network-free + unit-tested), absolutizes un-fetched refs so the archive degrades rather than breaks. `hiker-extract/src/builtin/web/archive.rs:build`; written to `<clip-stem>/original.html` companion folder (`sidecar.rs:write_clip_archive`). GAP: subresources referenced only inside CSS (`@font-face`/`background:url()`) are not yet inlined (direct `<img>/<source>`/stylesheet-link only). The artifact-retention cascade + `.hiker/refs/<doc_id>/` storage now landed in `extract-artifact-retention` (the archive is retained per-op there on `hiker refresh`) |
| `extract-web-fallbacks` | done | thin-page fallback chain: declared full-text RSS/Atom feed → AMP variant (`rel=amphtml`) → print view (`rel=alternate media=print`), first with real content wins. `hiker-extract/src/builtin/web/fallback.rs:candidates` selects+resolves the URLs; `web/mod.rs:try_fallbacks` re-fetches + re-extracts each in order, gated on `THIN_CONTENT` |
| `extract-web-versioned` | done | `hiker refresh` now re-fetches every scraped clip and lands the result on its EXISTING sidecar via the op-log instead of a fresh collision-suffixed clip. Seam: `hiker-extract/src/scrape.rs::re_extract_url` (re-fetch → routed body+archive, no write, `core`-free) + `refreshable_clips` (enumerate); the cli host (`cli/src/main.rs::cmd_refresh`/`refresh_one`) opens the op-log, bootstraps, and routes the body through `core::ops::op_writes::reextract` (`op-log-reextract-replace`, no-op on identical). Re-`scrape` of a changed source / re-crawl page / changed feed entry route through the same path (`cmd_crawl` reconciles `report.pages`, `cmd_feed_poll` reconciles `report.updated_children`); the app's re-extract-on-source-change (`app/src/extract.rs::reextract_existing`) also routes here. The `URL → sidecar` map stays stable (op-log preserves the path) so wikilinks don't break |
| `extract-version-oplog` | done | re-extraction versioning IS the op-log: `core::oplog::reextract_replace` (the `extractor` op on `accepted`) + the `core::ops::op_writes::reextract` selector wired into scrape/refresh (cli), crawl/feed re-extracts (cli), and the app source-change path. History/diff/restore/version-dropdown reuse the existing `core::activity`/`doc_history` surfaces — "versions" = op-log history filtered to extractor+user ops; no bespoke version store. Tested `ops/tests.rs::reextract_replaces_linked_skips_unlinked` (changed → one version, identical → no-op via injected body, no network) |
| `extract-artifact-retention` | done | `core/src/extract.rs` owns the cascade + the `.hiker/refs/<doc_id>/` store + pruning. `Retention::parse` (`latest`/`keep:N`/`forever`, fail-safe `Latest`); `resolve_retention(vault_default, override, per_source)` resolves the cascade lower-wins (per-source `hiker.artifact_retention` → per-crawl/feed/glob override → vault `[extract].artifact_retention`); `store_artifact` writes `.hiker/refs/<doc_id>/<op_id>/<file>` keyed by the producing op (device-local — never synced); `prune_refs` keeps the N newest per-op dirs (`Latest`→1, `Keep(n)`→n, `Forever`→unbounded). Wired in `cli/src/main.rs::refresh_one` (store+prune the clip's HTML archive after a new version lands, per-source override from `clip_artifact_retention`). Tested `extract.rs` (`parses_retention_levels`, `cascade_lower_level_wins`, `store_and_prune_to_latest_keeps_only_current`, `prune_keep_n_retains_n_newest`, `prune_forever_removes_nothing`). GAP: crawl/feed artifact store+prune share the cascade resolver but the cli only stores the scrape/refresh HTML archive so far |
| `extract-web-no-js-stance` | planned | explicit boundary: no JS execution in hiker. SPA-only / authenticated-XHR / server-paywall content served by source plugins (API-fetch) or the external manifest-import path, never an embedded engine |
| `scrape-cmd` | done | `cli/src/main.rs:cmd_scrape` / `cmd_refresh` thin adapters over `hiker-extract/src/scrape.rs:scrape`/`refresh`. `hiker scrape <url> [--into <folder>] [--vault <path>]` writes a visible `mode: clip` note in `[extract].clip_folder` (default `clips/`, `--into` override) + the offline HTML archive in its companion folder; `hiker refresh` re-fetches every scraped clip. Stamps `web-scrape` provenance, `author: imported`, `source_url`, `captured_at` (RFC-3339, `sidecar.rs:now_iso8601`) |

Tested with RSS-2.0 + Atom fixtures (`syndication.rs` tests) |

| `rss-guid-dedup` | done | cross-poll dedup via a companion-folder manifest (NOT frontmatter — keeps the user-facing note un-bloated): `hiker-extract/src/feed/manifest.rs::Manifest` (`.feed-manifest.json`, `guid → {child_file, content_hash, published}`). `feed/mod.rs::poll` classifies each entry: new guid → `write_entry_child`; known guid + changed body-hash → `rewrite_child` (overwrite in place, child filename preserved); unchanged → no-op. Tested: 2nd poll with one new + one unchanged → exactly one new child (`feed/tests.rs`) |

Pruned children go to **trash** (`<vault>/.hiker/trash/`, the dir `core::trash` owns), NOT silent delete — no `core` dep added (documented seam in the module doc). Body stays user-owned. CLI `hiker crawl` (`cli/src/main.rs::cmd_crawl`) remains. Form→Markdown additionally refreshes a stale-but-CLEAN cached buffer via `maybe_reload_clean_buffer` (dirty buffer left alone → unsaved markdown edits never discarded); Markdown→Form drops `loaded` so the form re-parses the (possibly hand-edited) frontmatter, and `persist` (Form-only, dirty-gated) never fires on the reload frame. `switch_view`/`ToggleReconcile` is the egui-free seam unit-tested in `capture/mod.rs::tests` |

Self-contained; JS runs only in the user's external tool (tested in `crawl/tests.rs`) |
| `ingest-two-lane-model` | planned | — | the boundary rule: no-JS internal *pull* (Lane A: clip/crawl/feed, `crawl-frontier-loop`) vs external *import* (Lane B, `extract-manifest-import`); both converge on one vault-integration spine. Framing section in `extract.md` |
| `ingest-output-kind` | planned | — | a note's extracted content displays as straight markdown OR a static HTML/CSS rendition; the HTML/CSS kind is Lane-B-only, Lane A is markdown-only; both always carry indexable text |
| `htmlview-render` | planned | `hiker-render/.../htmlview` (`hiker-htmlview`) | in-app no-JS HTML/CSS renderer for the HTML/CSS output kind; renders the static rendition only (the JS that built it ran in the external crawler). The renderer crate (`hiker-htmlview`, in the `hiker-render` submodule) is now in-tree, wired into the workspace, and already drives the ZIM viewer (host calls `layout`/`paint`/`link_at`); the remaining planned work for this slug is wiring it to the Lane-B captured-rendition output kind (`ingest-output-kind`). Renders the static rendition only; replaces the prior open-original-only posture for Lane-B captures |
| `import-format-contract` | planned | — | documented tool-agnostic import format (extends `extract-manifest-import`'s manifest with `html_file?`); any external crawler / OTS acquisition engine / hiker-crawler can target it |
| `extract-source-plugin-boundary` | planned | the unbounded byte→text tail (per-site scrapers, niche text formats) is `plugins.md`'s source-plugin surface, deliberately distinct from this binary-capable built-in registry |

## Feature registry (feature-registry.md)

Trait-based registry of feature descriptors that App-shell consumers (sidebar mode switcher, activity bar, hamburger menu, command palette, keybind registry) iterate to discover what to render. Lifts per-feature state ownership out of `PanelStates` to break the `panels <-> sidebar` cross-sibling coupling. Phased rollout — Phase 1 lands the foundation + the first feature (clusters); later phases migrate trails / boards / search / etc.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |

Surface vocabulary + `dispatch_action` (default `UnknownAction`) defined. Sub-trait *impls* beyond a stub `SidebarSurface` are still absent (see surface rows) |
| `feature-registry` | done | `app/src/feature/mod.rs` (`Registry::build`/`iter`/`by_id`/`invoke`), built in `app/src/bootstrap.rs:861`, stashed on `AppState::features`. Ordered `Vec<Arc<dyn Feature>>` via `builtin_features()`; plugin append (`feature-plugin-parity`) not yet wired |
| `feature-ctx` | done | `app/src/feature/mod.rs` — `Ctx<'a>` (`services`/`nav`/`toasts`/`config` + `state: &mut dyn Any`) + `with_ctx` disjoint-borrow builder. `with_ctx` resolves a state slice for `clusters`/`trails`/`vault` only |

Clusters/trails/vault state lifted onto `AppState`; everything else stays in `PanelStates` (by design during migration). Surfaces still reach through `&mut AppState`, not ctx-only |

| `feature-multi-region-sidebar` | partial | `egui-workbench/src/side_panel_stack.rs` — VSCode-style accordion. `SidePanelStack<Mode>` holds an ordered `sections: Vec<Mode>` + per-section `collapsed`/height `weights` + `focused`. Activity-bar click *switches* (focus if already open keeping the arrangement, else open in full — replace the whole stack with that one section; never adds to a split); clicking the focused icon hides the bar (`workspace.rs` click handler). Dragging an activity icon out of the strip and releasing it over the window *adds* that mode as a section (VSCode "drag a view into the sidebar"): `activity_bar.rs` reports `ActivityBarResponse::dropped_out` when a drag releases outside the strip rect, publishes a `drag_active_id()` flag mid-drag, and `side_panel_stack::paint_drop_target` lights up the bar while a drag hovers it. `Render` paints each section: a uniform header (twistie + title = drag handle for reorder + host action buttons; no per-section focus highlight, no `+`/`…` buttons) and, when expanded, the host `side_bar_ui` body. Right-clicking a header opens the panel menu: host `side_bar_actions_menu` + "Add panel" submenu (unopened modes) + "Close panel". Inter-section resize handles transfer height `weights` (clamped to `MIN_BODY`, guarded against the short-window `clamp` inversion). `Workbench::open_primary_panel` (switch) / `add_primary_panel` (add section) / `set_primary_panels` (restore) + `SidePanelStack::restore` / `open_modes` / `collapsed_modes` / `section_weight` / `focused` accessors. No egui_tiles, no tab strip — a lone section looks like the old single side bar. **Persistence:** `app/src/side_panel_persist.rs` writes the arrangement (open sections + collapsed + weights + focused + visibility) to `<vault>/.hiker/side-panel.json` (own `version`, currently 1) — captured + value-compared each autosave tick (no dirty flag since the accordion mutates inside egui_workbench), restored in `bootstrap::open_vault` after tab-state restore. Tests: `egui-workbench/tests/resize.rs` + `side_panel_stack::tests` (switch / split-restore). Drag-reorder + resize need a manual smoke check |

The `search.sections.*_expanded` settings are now inert |

The batch "Empty trash" verb lives in the header right-click menu (`Host::side_bar_actions_menu` for `Trash`, disabled when empty) and routes through the `EmptyTrash` confirm modal. Lands **as one unit with** `feature-vault-as-feature` — `sidebar/files.rs` (~85% vault concerns) *splits* (tree → shared crate, decorations → Vault host), it does not collapse to a thin wrapper. Model on hiker-lite's pure `panels/filetree.rs` (144 LOC) |
| `feature-hexview-shared` | planned | Phase 5: promote `hiker-lite/src/panels/hex_view.rs` (stateless `show(ui, &HexBuffer)`) → `hiker-features/src/hex_view/` with a tiny `HexViewCtx`; thin wrapper `Feature` in **both** hiker-lite and hiker-app. Simplest shared feature — no coupling to untangle. Currently no hex view exists in hiker-app at all |
| `feature-hiker-lite-adoption` | planned | Phase 5: hiker-lite uses the egui_workbench feature system. File search / find-replace / hex view become Features; reuses `hiker_features::filetree::render` (returns default `RowDecoration` + fs-rename `move_path` — exercises the pure-tree path). Activity bar + multi-region sidebar wire in. A `FileTreeCtx` method hiker-lite can't satisfy is the coupling alarm — fix in the shared crate, don't stub |
| `feature-vault-as-feature` | planned | Phase 4 (coupled to `feature-filetree-shared`): the Vault feature (`app/src/vault/`) *composes* `filetree::render` and layers every hiker decoration on top via `FileTreeCtx::decorate_row` (index markers, trail/board rows + menus) + supplies the referrer-rewriting `move_path`. Lands as a `Feature` from day one — no separate migration |


## Inbox rules (inbox-rules.md)

Deterministic, user-authored auto-org for newly-created notes. Sibling to the AI-driven Trees system. All planned.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `inbox-rules` | partial | first-match-wins rule list in `[inbox].rules` (per `settings-section-inbox`); each rule matches basename regex and/or first-4KB body regex, action is `move_to` (folder) and/or `add_tag` (frontmatter tag). Engine in `core/src/inbox.rs` (`Rules::compile` / `apply_to_created`), fired from the **indexer's** Created handler (`core/src/indexer/jobs.rs::run_inbox_rules`) for indexable extensions; routes through `move-note-core-cmd` + the frontmatter-merge primitive. Toast with Undo. Unaffected by `llm-features-disable-entirely`. **Partial: no UI** — rules are config-only (raw `[inbox].rules` TOML); no settings editor / rule-management surface yet |


## Vault view (vault-view.md)

A second sidebar mode presenting the vault as a read-only logical lens over the on-disk Files tree — the follow-on that makes crawl-job nesting and source grouping legible. All planned; lands after the crawl pipeline.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `vault-view-mode` | partial | `app/src/vault_view/` + `HikerMode::Vault` + `PANEL_VAULT` + the `vault` `Feature` (registry entry, safe-dial `Vault` icon). Sidebar mode reachable from the activity bar; `⋯` hosts the lens picker. **Gap:** mode choice not yet persisted to `vault.sidebar_mode` (resets to Files on relaunch) |
| `vault-view-readonly-lens` | done | `app/src/vault_view/mod.rs` renders a read-only tree — click opens (preview-slot / Mod-click sticky), no drag-to-reorganize, no placement state stored. Lenses: `Composed` (default, the metadata-derived groupings below) + by-folder (flattened) + flat, picked from `⋯` |
| `vault-view-crawl-nesting` | done | `app/src/vault_view/tree.rs:build_crawl_nodes` nests every note whose `hiker.parent` stamp resolves to a capture/manifest note's id under that note (`NodeKind::Capture`); a folder-mate with **no** stamp, or a dangling stamp, stays a normal node (tested: `crawl_children_nest_by_parent_stamp`, `dangling_parent_stamp_is_not_nested`). Stamp authority, not folder membership; data read once via `Store::notes_with_meta` |
| `vault-view-trail-nesting` | done | `app/src/vault_view/tree.rs:build_trail_nodes`+`nest_waypoints` nests a trail-doc's waypoints by the resolved `trail_waypoints` tree (`parent_waypoint_id` + `tree_path`, re-derived from `hiker.waypoints` on ingest), preserving order + side-trail branching (tested: `trail_waypoints_nest_with_side_trail_order`). Whole table read once via `Store::all_trail_waypoints` |
| `vault-view-sidecar-surfacing` | done | `app/src/vault_view/tree.rs:build_sidecar_nodes` detects `<src>.<ext>.md` sidecars (`sidecar_source`) and renders the non-md source as a synthetic node with the sidecar nested (tested: `sidecar_source_detection`, `sidecar_pairs_with_synthetic_source`). **GAP:** synthetic source node opens the source path via `editor_pane::open_file` rather than a Files-mode reveal verb — fine for indexed sidecars, "view original" verb is followup |
| `vault-view-source-groups` | done | `app/src/vault_view/tree.rs:build_source_groups` groups leftover notes by the authorship trichotomy (`hiker.author`/`hiker.provenance` via `author_bucket`); chat sessions collapse into a "Sessions" bucket with an "Imported" sub-bucket, labelled by date+id (`session_label`) not filename (tested: `source_groups_split_by_authorship_and_sessions`). **NOT blocked on a new index column** — the existing `note_meta` index (v8, `store-note-metadata-index`) already holds `hiker.author`/`provenance`/`parent`/`kind`; the lens reads them in one query (`core/src/store/metadata.rs:notes_with_meta`), no per-note disk reads, no schema bump |
| `vault-view-membership-groups` | planned | deferred — trail / cluster membership as additional Vault-mode groupings |
| `vault-view-saved-lenses` | planned | deferred — user-defined grouping rules (tag facet / folder glob / frontmatter field) saved and re-selectable |


## Wikilinks (wikilinks.md)

Path-based note references authored and stored as names. Stored form is `[[Name]]` (unique basename) or `[[folder/Name]]` (disambiguated). Existing ULID-form implementation needs rework: parsing, decoration, autocomplete, normalize-on-save, resolution, backlinks, and unresolved-handling all need to move from ULID-keyed to path-keyed. Rename now rewrites every referrer (wikilink bodies + trail/kanban paths) through a single shared pass.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `wikilink-path-form` | done | `core/src/wikilink.rs` (`parse_links`, `ParsedLink`) | parser drops ULID detection + `\|display` alias half; the body is the target verbatim (bare basename or `folder/sub/Name`). The save-time normalize pass was previously removed |
| `wikilink-render` | partial | `editor/editor-md/src/links.rs` (`wikilink_decorations()`); `app/src/panels/buffer/wikilink_nav.rs` (`title_resolver`) | decoration parses path-form bodies and renders the resolver-provided title (basename today); frontmatter `title` lookup deferred until the indexer surface for that lands |
| `wikilink-autocomplete` | done | `app/src/completion_sources.rs` (`WikilinkSource`) | inserts `wikilink::shortest_unambiguous(paths, rel)` — bare basename when vault-unique, minimal folder-prefix when not |
| `wikilink-hand-typed` | done | `core/src/wikilink.rs` (`resolve_path`) | bare-name + explicit-path resolution via `resolve_path`; no rewrite on save, no stamping. The click resolver in `wikilink_nav.rs` consumes it directly |
| `wikilink-ambiguous-resolution` | done | `core/src/wikilink.rs` (`AmbiguityPolicy`, `resolve_path`); `core/src/config/sections.rs` (`WikilinksConfig`, `AmbiguousResolution`) | vault-scope `[wikilinks] ambiguous_resolution = "unresolved" \| "lex-first" \| "nearest-folder"`, default `"unresolved"`. Lex-first sorts and picks first; nearest-folder counts shared path-segment prefix with the referrer |
| `wikilink-case-sensitivity` | planned | path equality follows host FS rules; cross-platform vaults inherit case-sensitive; sync layer warns on case-only collisions |
| `wikilink-rename-rewrite` | done | `core/src/links_rename.rs` (`on_note_moved` → trails + boards + wikilink-body sweep, `rewrite_wikilink_bodies` + `splice_link_bodies`); `core/src/indexer/jobs.rs` (single call site replaces the prior `run_trails_on_note_moved` / `run_boards_on_note_moved` pair on `IndexJob::Rename` / `Move` / `MoveFolder`); `core/src/trails/ops.rs` (`write_with_suppress_and_reindex_for_links` re-export) | shared rename-rewrite pass over all three referrer types runs as one orchestrator from the indexer; each domain is best-effort (errors logged, never propagated). Wikilink bodies use `wikilink::shortest_unambiguous` for the replacement form (same picker rule autocomplete uses). The Bloom-filter optimization is intentionally skipped (`wikilink-rename-bloom-filter-deferred`) — the straightforward `walk_indexable_files` enumerator lands first |
| `wikilink-rename-bloom-filter-deferred` | planned | deferred optimization: Bloom over "note contains any wikilinks" to skip the no-link majority on rename; build the straightforward enumerator first |
| `wikilink-resolve` | done | `core/src/wikilink.rs` (`resolve_path`) | bare-name matches every path with the matching basename; explicit-path matches the exact vault path; ambiguity routed through `AmbiguityPolicy` (config-driven). No `path_for_id` / `id_for_path` indirection. Backlinks scan + click flow consume this resolver directly |
| `wikilink-click-open` | partial | `app/src/panels/buffer/mod.rs` (`open_wikilink_at`) + `app/src/panels/buffer/wikilink_nav.rs` (`open_at`) | path-form click resolves through `wikilink::resolve_path` with the vault's `[wikilinks] ambiguous_resolution` policy and the referrer's path. The `WidgetClick` plumbing in `panels/buffer/mod.rs` itself is owned by the user's in-flight click-pattern refactor — left untouched |
| `wikilink-backlinks` | partial | `app/src/panels/backlinks.rs` (`scan_backlinks`) | scan now resolves every parsed link via `wikilink::resolve_path` and matches against the active path. Still O(total bytes) per scan — the spec'd reverse-index (`structural index records source-path → target-path edges`) lands with `wikilink-rename-rewrite` |
| `wikilink-unresolved` | partial | `editor/editor-md/src/links.rs` (`COLOR_WIKILINK_UNRESOLVED`); `app/src/panels/buffer/wikilink_nav.rs` (`open_at` → `create_and_open`) | unresolved style + create-on-click stays; ambiguous-under-unresolved currently surfaces as a toast ("Multiple notes named …") — the disambiguation picker UI is the remaining gap |
| `wikilink-headings-blocks` | planned | deferred — `[[Name#Heading]]` / `[[Name#^block]]` (block needs an auto-injected anchor in the target) |
| `wikilink-embed` | planned | deferred — `![[Name]]` transclusion rendering the target's content/image/PDF inline |
| `wikilink-hover-preview` | done | `app/src/panels/buffer/wikilink_nav.rs` (`HoverState`, `track_hover`, `resolve_preview`) + `app/src/panels/graph.rs` (`paint_preview_card_with` scrollable variant) | ~400ms hover on a resolved pill shows a scrollable preview card painted on a tooltip-layer over the editor. Title is frontmatter `title` if present, else basename; body is the first ~30 lines after `skip_frontmatter`. Pointer-leave dismisses after a 200ms grace (covers pill→card slide). Scroll wheel over the card scrolls only the card body. Unresolved / ambiguous pills get no card. Mod-click closes the card and opens the target sticky via `wikilink-click-open`. Reuses `paint_preview_card` styling shared with `cluster-editor-graph-view-hover-preview-card` |
| `url-classify` | partial | `core/src/url.rs` (`classify`, `LinkTarget`, `resolve_path`) | pure, store-free, UI-free link-shape classifier — the single source of truth for "what kind of target is this link string?". Distinguishes `External` (`http(s)`/`mailto:`), `Zim { archive, article }` (mirrors `zim.rs`'s `zim://<authority>/<rest>` shape), `VaultPath` (slash- or known-extension-shaped), and `Wikilink` (`[[…]]` or bare name, alias/`#section` stripped). Precedence: brackets → external scheme → `zim://` → path shape → bare name. Optional `resolve_path` helper takes the existence/name lookups as closures so the module stays store-free. `app/src/panels/zim.rs::resolve_href` routes its external-scheme bail-out through `classify`; the interactive-diagram click path (`widget-mermaid-links`) consumes both `classify` and `resolve_path` via `diagram_nav::dispatch_link`. Remaining: route the wikilink pill click path through it too (still on `wikilink::resolve_path` directly) |


## Plugins (plugins.md)

| Slug | Status | Evidence | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | -------- | ----- |
| `plugin-host` | done | `core/src/plugins/mod.rs` (`PluginHost`), `core/src/plugins/error.rs` | Orchestrates load → verify → instantiate → init; holds loaded instances + latest VDOM per plugin; `load_enabled` reads `plugins.json`. No `pub use` re-export farm |
| `plugin-runtime-wasmi` | done | `core/src/plugins/runtime.rs` (`WasmEngine` trait + `WasmiEngine`) | Swappable engine trait; `wasmi` pure-Rust interpreter is the only impl, confined here. Chosen over wasmtime for clean SBOM (no JIT); wasmtime/Component-Model is the future swap |
| `plugin-host-call` | done | `core/src/plugins/runtime.rs` (`host_call_shim` + fat-pipe marshalling), `core/src/plugins/dispatch.rs` (`HostInvoker`, `HostApi`, `StoreHostApi`) | `host_call(name,args_json)->result_json` over linear memory (plugin exports `memory`+`plugin_alloc`, packed `ptr<<32\|len`). `notes.query` wraps `store-note-query`; `notes.list` + `log.*` also wired |
| `plugin-permissions` | done | `core/src/plugins/manifest.rs` (`Permissions`), `core/src/plugins/dispatch.rs` (`required_permission`, `HostInvoker::invoke`) | Capability-scoped, fail-closed: each host call maps to a required permission; missing grant or unknown call is refused (delivered to the plugin as a JSON error, not silent). `log.*` is free |
| `plugin-manifest` | done | `core/src/plugins/manifest.rs` (`Manifest`, `UiDecl`, `SidebarPanelDecl`) | `manifest.json` schema: id/name/version/permissions/ui/entry; immutable per identity |
| `plugin-hash-pin` | done | `core/src/plugins/manifest.rs` (`PluginsFile`, `PinnedEntry`, `blake3_pin`, `verify_pin`) | `plugins.json` pins manifest + wasm by blake3; a mismatch aborts load (no running changed code against an old pin) |
| `plugin-vdom` | done | `core/src/plugins/vdom.rs` (`Node`, `Row`, `TextStyle`) | Declarative VDOM the plugin returns; bounded primitive set (vstack/hstack/text/text_input/button/note_link/list/divider/spacer). Host renders natively |
| `plugin-resource-limits` | done | `core/src/plugins/runtime.rs` (`FUEL_PER_CALL`, `consume_fuel` + per-call `set_fuel`) | Fuel budget per entry call so a runaway plugin traps. Memory cap + host-call timeout still to layer on |
| `plugin-vdom-egui-renderer` | done | `app/src/panels/plugin_panel.rs` (`show_loaded` + `render_node`), `app/src/panels/plugins.rs` (renders loaded panels above the manifest editor), `app/src/state.rs` + `app/src/bootstrap.rs` (`VaultSession.plugins`, `build_plugin_host`) | egui renderer for the VDOM `Node` tree; element events route via `PluginHost::dispatch_event`, `note_link` clicks resolve through `path_for_id` → `editor_pane::open_file` |
| `plugin-notes-query-example` | done | `plugins/notes-query/` (excluded from the workspace; host-target logic tests pass) | dataview-style plugin: `build_query_args` / `build_vdom` pure logic + `#[cfg(wasm32)]` ABI shim; manifest + README with the `wasm32-unknown-unknown` build + `plugins.json` hash-pin install. Needs the wasm toolchain to produce the artifact |
| `plugin-install-flow` | done | `app/src/panels/plugins.rs` (master/detail manager) | Install-from-folder picks a plugin dir (`rfd`), parses + blake3-pins manifest + wasm, presents the requested permissions for consent, and on accept copies it under `.hiker/plugins/<id>/` + writes the `plugins.json` entry (disabled). Left list of installed plugins (+ pending review) with status dots; right detail shows manifest, a plain-language permissions list (network/write tinted), status, and Enable / Disable / Reload / Remove (live load/unload via `PluginHost`). A loaded plugin's panel renders inline in the detail |
| `plugin-manager-detail-panel` | done | `app/src/panels/plugins.rs` (`detail_installed`), `app/src/panels/plugin_panel.rs` (`render_plugin`) | The selected loaded plugin's live VDOM panel renders in the detail pane under its info/permissions/actions |
| `plugin-runtime-field` | planned | — | Manifest `runtime` field selects the tier: `wasm` (prebuilt) or `lua` (script); both share manifest/permissions/hash-pin/`host_call`/VDOM/`tools` |
| `plugin-runtime-lua` | planned | — | Bundled reference C-Lua compiled freestanding to wasm32 (no WASI, `os`/`io`/`package` stripped), run under `wasmi`; vendored build artifact, no runtime dep. `piccolo`/`mlua` rejected |
| `plugin-script-source` | planned | — | `lua`-tier plugins ship source + manifest; `load_script(ptr,len)` export feeds source; hash-pin covers source; interpreter versioned with hiker; manifest records `lua_version` |
| `plugin-script-require` | planned | — | `require`/multi-file Lua resolves via a custom searcher over the plugin bundle's files, never the filesystem |
| `plugin-no-host-compile` | planned | — | Hiker never compiles, bundles no compiler, assumes no toolchain; compilation lives in the author's env (`wasm` tier) or is avoided (`lua` tier) |
| `plugin-mcp-tools` | planned | — | Manifest `tools` (`{name,description,input_schema}`) registers MCP tools dispatched into the plugin instance; advertised via `mcp-dynamic-capabilities` while enabled |
| `plugin-mcp-tools-permission` | planned | — | Providing MCP tools requires `provide:mcp-tool` permission, distinct from the consume-side `mcp:invoke` |
| `plugin-host-mcp-seam` | planned | — | `mcp-server` holds a `PluginHost` handle and calls one `dispatch_tool(plugin_id, tool, args)` edge; mirrors the `IndexerHandle` boundary, no internals crossing |
| `plugin-authoring-proposal-gate` | planned | — | Agent-authored plugins enter as proposals through `plugin-install-flow`, never auto-enabled; human consent + sandbox + approved caps bound the blast radius |
| `plugin-source-extractor` | planned | — | source plugin = URL/bytes→markdown fetcher/transform (not a binary extractor); registers into `extract.md`'s registry, returns `{markdown, frontmatter?}` into the same sidecar/versioned write path, stamps `imported` |
| `plugin-source-matcher` | planned | — | manifest declares a URL-pattern / extension matcher; advertised to the extractor registry after built-ins (built-ins win on overlap unless `hiker.extractor` names the plugin) |
| `plugin-source-api-fetch` | planned | — | per-site scraper handles a client-rendered site by calling its backing JSON API directly via `net:<host>` + `http.fetch` — does what the page's JS would, without executing it |
| `plugin-source-permission` | planned | — | `provide:source-extractor` capability, distinct from UI/tool caps; a scraper additionally requests the specific `net:<host>` it fetches |
| `plugin-source-cache-version` | planned | — | manifest `version` feeds `extract-version-cache-key`; extraction cached per source-version, so `wasmi`'s no-JIT slowness is paid once — for the per-site/per-format tail, not bulk reprocessing |
| `plugin-source-defensive-parse` | planned | — | parsing hostile input in the fuel-bounded sandbox (`plugin-resource-limits`) is safer than native in-process parsing; a security gain on top of portability |
| `plugin-authoring-fetch-raw` | planned | — | authoring-loop tool: pull a sample page's raw HTML (+ data-blobs) so the agent sees the structure; user-supplied/confirmed URL (caps fetch reach). Specced in `mcp.md` as `mcp-fetch-raw` |
| `plugin-authoring-extract-preview` | planned | — | authoring-loop tool: run a candidate extractor against a URL/file → `{markdown,next_urls}` without vault writes; runs in the `wasmi` sandbox so it's safe to invoke freely. `mcp.md` `mcp-extract-preview` |
| `plugin-authoring-security` | planned | — | host-enforced scoped sub-task (fetch+preview+propose, no vault-write/arbitrary-net) + sandboxed preview + user-confirmed fetch + human gate at install; defends by consequence, not content review. Generalization parked as `agent-session-partitions` |

Hovered resize square and connector circle grow `HOVER_GROW` = 1.3x about their center; the hovered group's header band brightens (`gamma_multiply` 0.22→0.4). Purely visual, no state change. `grown_about_center` unit-tested (grows + stays centered, identity at 1.0) |
| `canvas-lod-placeholder` | done | `hiker-canvas/view/src/paint.rs` (`is_tiny`/`lod_title`/`paint_lod_placeholder`, `node_card` branch), `hiker-canvas/view/src/widget.rs` (`handle_zoom` wheel routing) | below ~150px on-screen a card skips the content engine and paints a cheap title + skeleton placeholder; collapsed zoom-to-fit on 121 nodes from 81ms to 0.04ms/frame (profiled via `tools/profile-canvas`). A wheel over a placeholder (no scrollable content) passes through to camera zoom via the shared `is_tiny` check, so a tiny card crossing the cursor mid-zoom-out doesn't stall the zoom |
| `canvas-inline-edit` | done | `app/src/panels/canvas/edit.rs` (overlay + `EDIT_VIEWS` thread-local + `forget`/`enter`/`is_editable`/`show_overlay`); `render.rs::activate_or_edit` / `resolve_edit_overlay` / `render_edit_overlay` / `persist_text_edit`; `Pane::editing` in `mod.rs`; `widget.rs::node_screen_rect` / `is_node_lod`; `buffer_view.rs` edit-preview popup + `EmbedOpts::focus`; `edit::forget` in `editor_pane::close_tab` | cards editable in place: double-click a full-detail File/Text card → foreground `Area` overlay over the node rect (captures kbd+pointer, tracks rect via `node_screen_rect` on pan/zoom; Esc / click-out / select-other / off-screen exit). Overlay seeds at the card's current scroll (`card_scroll`), no jump to top. Save routing (`handle_canvas_save`): Ctrl/Cmd+S while editing a File node saves that note's buffer; otherwise (incl. a Text node) it commits the canvas document. File node → edits the shared note buffer via `embedded-buffer-view` (typing shows in any tab, one dirty buffer); Text node → transient editor reconciled to its own `text` via `canvas-edit-ops` `SetText` through the `persist_canvas` op-log path. Per-edit `EmbeddedView` / transient `Editor` parked in a `TabId`-keyed thread-local (off `AppState`) so `show_embedded_buffer` holds `&mut app` + `&mut embed` without aliasing — mirrors `content::PANES`. Read path unchanged (cards read live `working` buffer; LOD placeholder double-click opens a tab via the existing `activate_node`). Diagram preview-while-typing added to the `embedded-buffer-view` primitive (disjoint `buffers[path]` immut / `panels.edit_preview` mut), so every embed gets it; the buffer tab doesn't use the primitive, so no double-fire. Verified: `cargo test -p hiker-app -p canvas-view` 237 pass; clippy clean in touched files (`-D` budget lints); `profile-canvas` zoom-to-fit 0.06ms p50 |

## hiker-crawler (hiker-crawler.md)

The JS-capable companion app. Separate, excluded-from-workspace crate; output
always lands through an existing hiker seam (crawl-job note / source plugin /
manifest-import). The shared extractions (`hiker-llm`, `hiker-theme`,
`hiker-extract` public surface) and the crawler itself are built: the
`egui_workbench` shell + multi-tab browsing, the CEF engine (OSR render + input,
DevTools `rendered_html`/picker, CDP `Network` WARC), the element picker, the
three emit targets, and LLM authoring are all implemented and build under the
`cef` feature (clippy-clean; default `NullEngine` build also clean). **`done`
here means implemented + builds + clippy-clean — the GUI has not been run in the
dev env (no display), so runtime verification is the user's step.** `crawl-run`
stays `partial` (UI-blocking v1, allow-all robots); `render-preview` /
`html-output` stay `planned` (await the custom renderer).

| Slug | Status | Evidence | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | -------- | ----- |
| `crawler-app-shell` | done | `hiker-crawler/src/main.rs`, `hiker-crawler/src/app.rs` | separate egui/eframe binary on the shared `egui_workbench` shell; excluded from the root workspace like `plugins/` (CEF bundles a Chromium distribution + C++ FFI binding, a separate heavy build that must not block `check.sh`) |
| `crawler-browser-tabs` | done | `hiker-crawler/src/app.rs` (workbench + tabs), `hiker-crawler/src/engine.rs` (CEF runtime vs per-tab browser) | CEF is windowless (no chrome of its own); each page is an `egui_workbench` tab backed by one windowless browser, tab strip drives new/close/switch. Global CEF runtime (init/subprocess/pump) split from per-tab browser (load/paint/input/DevTools) |
| `crawler-quarantine` | done | `hiker-crawler/Cargo.toml` (no hiker crate depends on it) | a full Chromium + open-web fetch + C++ CEF + CEF's multi-process model live ONLY here; `hiker-core`/`extract`/`app` keep `extract-web-no-js-stance` and the `deny.toml` clean-SBOM posture. The crawler's user accepts the browser baggage by choosing it |
| `crawler-share-code` | done | `hiker-llm/`, `hiker-theme/` (extractions landed); `hiker-crawler/Cargo.toml` (deps point down only) | standing rule: behavior shared between hiker and the crawler is shared *code* in a common leaf crate, never a second impl; logic trapped in a binary (`hiker-app`) or too-heavy crate (`hiker-core`) is extracted down, not duplicated. Crawler never depends on `hiker-app`. Reuses `egui_workbench`, `hiker_extract::crawl::{PageSource,run,CrawlParams,write_job_note,manifest}`, `editor-egui` as-is |
| `crawler-shared-llm` | done | `hiker-llm/src/lib.rs` (client); `core/src/llm.rs` (`client_from_config`/`provider_config_from` bridge); consumers in `app/src/{bootstrap,chat/*,clusters/sidebar}`, `mcp-server/src/agent_bridge.rs` | LLM client lifted from `core/src/llm.rs` into `hiker-llm`, consumed by core *and* (later) crawler; `llm` crate removed from core; config-bridge fns stayed in core (`pub_use` denied so import sites were repointed). Same client, not a copy |
| `crawler-shared-theme` | done | `hiker-theme/src/lib.rs`; ~40 app call sites repointed (`use hiker_theme as theme`) | `app/src/theme.rs` lifted into `hiker-theme`; app + crawler share colors/fonts/spacing. `egui_workbench`/`editor_core` themes left untouched |
| `crawler-shared-render` | done | `hiker-crawler/Cargo.toml` (`hiker-htmlview` path dep into the `hiker-render` submodule), `hiker-crawler/src/preview.rs` (`RenderedPreview`) | crawler depends on the shared `hiker-render` crate (`hiker_htmlview`) and previews the HTML/CSS rendition through the same renderer hiker core uses (`htmlview-render`), so the rendition is WYSIWYG across the boundary. `RenderedPreview` owns the `!Send` `HtmlView`; subresources served offline from the engine's captured wire responses (shared with `crawler-warc-archive`) |
| `crawler-shared-chat` | planned | — | lift hiker-app's chat panel + agent loop (`core::agent`/`core::acp`, `llm.md`) into a leaf crate both share, so source-plugin authoring is an iterative author→preview→rework conversation (authoring-scoped tools only, `mcp-authoring-scoped-subtask`); heavier extraction than `hiker-llm` (entangled with `core::mcp` + sessions, must keep `hiker-core` unlinked from the crawler) |
| `crawler-engine-trait` | done | `hiker-crawler/src/engine.rs` (`BrowserEngine`, `NullEngine`) | engine behind a trait; CEF is a `cef`-feature impl, `NullEngine` default so the shell runs without a browser. Same seam discipline as `WasmEngine` / `ResourceProvider`; leaves room for a CDP-external back end later |
| `crawler-cef-engine` | done | `hiker-crawler/src/engine.rs` (`cef_impl::CefEngine`, `cef` feature), `hiker-crawler/src/main.rs` (subprocess entry), `hiker-crawler/src/app.rs` (texture upload + input) | **Render milestone done + links** (`cargo build --features cef`): windowless OSR, self-exec subprocess handling, external message-loop pump, `OnPaint` BGRA → egui texture (CPU path, no wgpu), mouse/scroll/keyboard forwarding. cef 148.3.0 on aarch64. `rendered_html`/`pick_at` via DevTools and WARC via the CDP `Network` tap are now wired (see `crawler-element-picker` / `crawler-warc-archive`). GUI not yet run here (no display) — runtime check is the user's step |
| `crawler-element-picker` | done | `hiker-crawler/src/picker.rs`, `hiker-crawler/src/engine.rs` (`PICK_JS_BODY` repeat flag, `highlight_selector`/`clear_highlight`), `hiker-crawler/src/app.rs` (`sync_highlight`, hover wiring) | click the live page → ranked selector candidates (id / class / attr / nth-child) + node HTML/text; named fields form the extractor spec. Repeat/list detection live (`querySelectorAll(top).length>1` → `Hit.repeat` → `Field.repeat` → `Selection::has_repeat`); side-panel hover injects an outline CSS via `execute_javascript` (cleared on un-hover), TODO note re unverified without a display |
| `crawler-link-strategy` | done | `hiker-crawler/src/app.rs` (per-job link-following control) | per-job choice — static list (pasted / repeat-selector harvest over the JS-rendered page) / dynamic discovery (scope patterns + depth) / plugin-driven `next_urls` — templated into crawl-job `mode`+`depth`+scope frontmatter. Maps onto `crawl-modes`; hiker's frontier loop unchanged. Static-list harvest is a JS→no-JS bridge (freeze links that only exist post-JS) |
| `crawler-emit-targets` | done | `hiker-crawler/src/emit.rs` | one selection set → three outputs, all existing hiker seams |
| `crawler-emit-crawl-job` | done | `hiker-crawler/src/emit.rs` (crawl-job emitter) | template seed + scope + extractor into a `mode: crawl` capture-spec note (`crawl-job-note`) |
| `crawler-emit-source-plugin` | done | `hiker-crawler/src/emit.rs` (plugin emitter) | generate a Lua/wasm source plugin (`plugin-source-extractor`) tuned to the site; installs via `plugin-install-flow` |
| `crawler-emit-mode` | done | `hiker-crawler/src/emit.rs` (`source_plugin` LLM branch), `hiker-crawler/src/llm_author.rs`, `hiker-crawler/src/app.rs` ("Source plugin (LLM)" button) | deterministic (template selectors) vs LLM-assisted authoring; deterministic default + offline. LLM path builds a `hiker_llm::ProviderConfig` from env (`HIKER_CRAWLER_LLM_*`, Anthropic defaults; no `hiker-core` dep), prompts the shared `hiker_llm::Client::chat` for a per-site Lua extractor via a current-thread tokio `block_on`. Missing API key → clear message; UI-blocking v1 (TODO to background). The iterative author→preview→rework upgrade rides the shared chat loop (`crawler-shared-chat`) |
| `crawler-direct-warc` | done | `hiker-crawler/src/emit.rs` (manifest emitter) | render → extract via shared `hiker-extract` → WARC + `manifest.json` dir; imported by `hiker-extract::crawl::manifest::import_dir` (`extract-manifest-import`) |
| `crawler-warc-archive` | done | `hiker-crawler/src/warc.rs` (assembly), `hiker-crawler/src/engine.rs` (`Capture` state, `Network.enable`, `on_dev_tools_event` tap, `drain_body_fetches`, `capture_warc`) | true wire-level WARC capture via CDP `Network` (`responseReceived` metadata + `getResponseBody` bodies, keyed by requestId, reset on navigation), assembled as warcinfo + response records by the pure-Rust `warc` crate (cef-gated, optional dep). Higher fidelity than `extract-web-archive-singlefile`'s DOM re-serialization. TODO: bodies evicted before the async `getResponseBody` round-trip lands are skipped. NullEngine `capture_warc` stays `None`. Faithful in-app WARC viewing still out of scope |
| `crawler-preview-fidelity` | done | `hiker-crawler/src/preview.rs` | preview reuses `hiker-extract`'s readability + `htmd` on the rendered DOM → byte-identical to hiker's ingest output. Needs a public `extract_from_html(html, base_url)` exposed (today `to_article`/`best_body` are `pub(super)`) |
| `crawler-crawl-run` | partial | `hiker-crawler/src/crawl_source.rs` (`CefPageSource`), `hiker-crawler/src/app.rs` (`run_crawl`/`run_crawl_to`, "In-app crawl run" button) | full in-app run: `CefPageSource: hiker_extract::crawl::PageSource` drives a dedicated crawl `CefBrowser` (load → bounded `runtime.pump()`+`browser.poll()` until rendered HTML + idle, iteration cap) → shared `extract_from_html`. Button builds `crawl_params(selection)` and calls `crawl::run(...)` to a chosen dir, reporting captured-page count. Governance reused verbatim; allow-all robots (no HTTP client in-crate). Default build keeps the NullEngine stub (no progress). UI-blocking v1 (TODO to background) |
| `crawler-render-preview` | done | `hiker-crawler/src/app.rs` ("Rendered preview (hiker-render)" button + output-pane render), `hiker-crawler/src/preview.rs` (`RenderedPreview::show`), `hiker-crawler/src/engine.rs` (`CefBrowser::captured_resources`) | "Rendered preview" snapshots the engine's rendered DOM + captured wire resources into a `hiker_htmlview::HtmlView` shown in the controls pane in place of the text output (host-driven layout/paint, like the ZIM viewer), so the rendition is verified against the same renderer the vault displays it with (`htmlview-render`). Default build (NullEngine) shows the build-with-cef hint. Followup: relative-URL resolution is exact-match against captured URLs for now (full base-resolution rides `crawler-html-output`) |
| `crawler-html-output` | planned | — | crawler emits a curated static HTML/CSS rendition (`html_file` in `import-format-contract`) alongside markdown for layout-rich captures, shown via hiker-render (`ingest-output-kind` / `htmlview-render`); still derives indexable text. Distinct from the WARC archive |
| `crawler-api-fetch-discovery` | planned | — | author an API-fetch source plugin (`plugin-source-api-fetch`) from observed traffic: correlate each picked field to the CDP Network response that produced it (`crawler-warc-archive` tap), target that endpoint directly instead of the post-JS DOM. Deterministic correlation narrows; agent path robustifies |
| `crawler-traffic-redaction` | planned | — | strip cookies/auth headers/token-shaped query params from captured traffic before any agent/LLM authoring sees it, so the crawler never ships the user's session secrets to a model |
| `crawler-validation-gate` | planned | — | before handoff, do a real no-JS fetch and run the candidate plugin/selectors against it, diffing vs the post-JS picks; classifies the site (selectors-survive / API-callable / needs-JS-auth) and emits a no-JS artifact only when base hiker can reproduce it |
| `crawler-handoff` | done | `hiker-crawler/src/emit.rs` | every output is an existing seam (capture-note frontmatter / installed plugin / manifest-import dir); the crawler is a front end, not a parallel ingestion path |


## Ideas (ideas.md)

Deferred ideas with named slugs but no spec doc of their own yet. Each row points at the `ideas.md` entry for context; a real spec doc will land if/when the feature is picked up.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `browser-extension-capture` | planned | companion browser extension with two primary buttons — "Save to Hiker" (one-click capture into `inbox/` via the existing scrape/extractor pipeline) and "Save to Hiker and append to active trail" (same capture, plus append as waypoint to the active trail, greyed when no active trail). Talks to the running app over the existing localhost MCP server with a new `scrape` tool; no new transport. Phone share-sheet / Android intent capture is the sibling future on mobile. Anchor: `ideas.md` "Capture surfaces" |
| `drawio-source-ingest` | planned | future ingestion source — `.drawio` (and `.drawio.svg` / `.drawio.png`) diagrams as a source type; structural skeleton (nodes, edges, labels) extracted into a derived hiker note for search/related-notes coverage; diagram file kept as canonical artifact. Anchor: `ideas.md` "Source ingestion" |
| `drawio-mcp-tools` | planned | future MCP tool family for drawio diagrams — `get_diagram` / `add_node` / `add_edge` / `update_node`; lets an attached agent read and incrementally edit the diagram file in place via the drawio XML format. Layout round-tripping is the hard part; v1 likely restricted to structural-only edits. Anchor: `ideas.md` "Source ingestion" |
| `cluster-chunk-thread-hint` | planned | parallel chunk-level clustering: tightly-clustered chunks across notes surface as "thread" hints (not auto-trails). Anchor: `ideas.md` "Clustering" |
| `cluster-chunk-multitopic-flag` | planned | parallel chunk-level clustering: a note whose chunks scatter across clusters is a split candidate. Anchor: `ideas.md` "Clustering" |
| `servo-warc-capture` | planned | high-fidelity WARC capture+replay above the single-file HTML archive (`extract-web-archive-singlefile`); full dynamic capture needs the external manifest-import path; md sidecar stays search, WARC the fidelity layer under versioned sources, replayed in an external viewer. Anchor: `ideas.md` "Viewers" |
| `agent-session-partitions` | planned | enforced partitions so no agent session holds all three prompt-injection trifecta legs at once (untrusted content + vault-write + arbitrary-net); generalization of `plugin-authoring-security`. Touches `mcp.md`/chat/`task-queue.md`/`plugins.md`; own spec when picked up. Anchor: `ideas.md` "Agent session capabilities" |
